DPO 逐题对照

LoRA SFT lora-v1 → DPO dpo-v2 · 固定贪心生成 · EvalPlus 原始测试与新增测试均通过才算通过。

仅分析已有结果。人工分类描述失败现象，不证明训练根因。空失败列表不代表没有失败，也不能据此认定是运行超时。JSON 中的元组可能显示为数组，不据此推断原始测试参数类型。

评测集退化改善均通过均失败
humaneval10247258
mbpp2617211124

推荐先看退化题，再切换改善题。原始 completion 是模型输出；solution 是被评分的完整代码。题目只供诊断，不加入训练。

HumanEval/11 · 退化 语法与无关续写

入口函数：string_xor

人工静态审阅：目标 string_xor 的实现与 SFT 一样，但 DPO 继续生成多个无关函数，最后的三引号字符串未闭合，整个模块无法解析。可确认的是语法错误；是否达到 token 上限尚未核实。

from typing import List


def string_xor(a: str, b: str) -> str:
    """ Input are two strings a and b consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result also as a string.
    >>> string_xor('010', '110')
    '100'
    """

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 422 字符（不是 token 数）

    return ''.join(str(int(x) ^ int(y)) for x, y in zip(a, b))


def string_xor_list(a: List[str], b: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    """ Input are two lists of strings a and b consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result also as a list of strings.
    >>> string_xor_list(['010', '110'], ['100', '000'])
    ['110', '010']
    """
    return [string_xor(x, y) for x, y in zip(a, b)]
完整被评分代码 solution
from typing import List


def string_xor(a: str, b: str) -> str:
    """ Input are two strings a and b consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result also as a string.
    >>> string_xor('010', '110')
    '100'
    """
    return ''.join(str(int(x) ^ int(y)) for x, y in zip(a, b))


def string_xor_list(a: List[str], b: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    """ Input are two lists of strings a and b consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result also as a list of strings.
    >>> string_xor_list(['010', '110'], ['100', '000'])
    ['110', '010']
    """
    return [string_xor(x, y) for x, y in zip(a, b)]

def string_xor_list(a: List[str], b: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    """ Input are two lists of strings a and b consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result also as a list of strings.
    >>> string_xor_list(['010', '110'], ['100', '001'])
    ['110', '011']
    """
    return [string_xor(x, y) for x, y in zip(a, b)]


def string_xor_list_to_string(a: List[str]) -> str:
    """ Input is a list of strings a consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result as a string.
    >>> string_xor_list_to_string(['010', '110'])
    '100'
    """
    return ''.join(string_xor_list(a)[i] for i in range(len(a)))


def string_xor_list_to_string_list(a: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    """ Input is a list of strings a consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result as a list of strings.
    >>> string_xor_list_to_string_list(['010', '110'])
    ['110', '011']
    """
    return [string_xor_list_to_string(a)[i] for i in range(len(a))]


def string_xor_list_to_string_list_to_string(a: List[str]) -> str:

    >>> string_xor_list_to_string_list_to_string(['010', '110'])
    '100'
    """
    return ''.join(string_xor_list_to_string_list(a)[i] for i in range(len(a)))

from typing import List


def string_xor(a: str, b: str) -> str:
    """ Input are two strings a and b consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result also as a string.
    >>> string_xor('010', '110')
    '100'
    """
    return ''.join(str(int(x) ^ int(y)) for x, y in zip(a, b))


def string_xor_list(a: List[str], b: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    """ Input are two lists of strings a and b consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result also as a list of strings.
    >>> string_xor_list(['010', '110'], ['100', '001'])
    ['110', '011']
    """
    return [string_xor(x, y) for x, y in zip(a, b)]


def string_xor_list_to_string(a: List[str]) -> str:
    """ Input is a list of strings a consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result as a string.
    >>> string_xor_list_to_string(['010', '110'])
    '100'
    """
    return ''.join(string_xor_list(a)[i] for i in range(len(a)))


def string_xor_list_to_string_list(a: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    """ Input is a list of strings a consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result as a list of strings.
    >>> string_xor_list_to_string_list(['010', '110'])
    ['110', '011']
    """
    return [string_xor_list_to_string(a)[i] for i in range(len(a))]


def string_xor_list_to_string_list_to_string(a: List[str]) -> str:
    """ Input is a list of strings a consisting only of 1s and 0s.
    Perform binary XOR on these inputs and return result as a string.
    >>> string_xor_list_to_string_list_to_string(['010', '110'])
    '100'
    """
    return ''.join(string_xor_list_to_string_list(a)[i] for i in range(len(a)))

人工静态审阅：concatenate 的主体仍是正确的 join；后面追加了 reverse、count_* 等无关函数，最终留下未闭合 docstring，导致整段 solution 语法失败。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
from typing import List


def concatenate(strings: List[str]) -> str:
    """ Concatenate list of strings into a single string
    >>> concatenate([])
    ''
    >>> concatenate(['a', 'b', 'c'])
    'abc'
    """

完整被评分代码 solution
from typing import List


def concatenate(strings: List[str]) -> str:
    """ Concatenate list of strings into a single string
    >>> concatenate([])
    ''
    >>> concatenate(['a', 'b', 'c'])
    'abc'
    """
    return ''.join(strings)

def reverse(string: str) -> str:
    """ Reverse a string
    >>> reverse('abc')
    'cba'
    """
    return string[::-1]


def is_palindrome(string: str) -> bool:
    """ Check if a string is a palindrome
    >>> is_palindrome('abc')
    False
    >>> is_palindrome('abba')
    True
    """
    return string == reverse(string)


def count_vowels(string: str) -> int:
    """ Count the number of vowels in a string
    >>> count_vowels('abc')
    0
    >>> count_vowels('abba')
    4
    """
    vowels = 'aeiouAEIOU'
    return sum(1 for char in string if char in vowels)


def count_consonants(string: str) -> int:
    """ Count the number of consonants in a string
    >>> count_consonants('abc')
    3
    >>> count_consonants('abba')
    2
    """
    consonants = 'bcdfghjklmnpqrstvwxyzBCDFGHJKLMNPQRSTVWXYZ'
    return sum(1 for char in string if char in consonants)


def count_words(string: str) -> int:
    """ Count the number of words in a string
    >>> count_words('abc')
    1
    >>> count_words('abba')
    2
    """
    return len(string.split())


def count_characters(string: str) -> int:
    """ Count the number of characters in a string
    >>> count_characters('abc')
    3
    >>> count_characters('abba')
    4
    """
    return len(string)


def count_uppercase(string: str) -> int:
    """ Count the number of uppercase characters in a string
    >>> count_uppercase('abc')
    0
    >>> count_uppercase('ABBA')
    4
    """
    return sum(1 for char in string if char.isupper())


def count_lowercase(string: str) -> int:
    """ Count the number of lowercase characters in a string
    >>> count_lowercase('abc')
    3
    >>> count_lowercase('ABBA')
    4
    """
    return sum(1 for char in string if char.islower())

HumanEval/36 · 退化 算法逻辑

入口函数：fizz_buzz

人工静态审阅：题目要求统计数字 7 出现的次数。DPO 用是否包含 7 代替 count('7')，77 中两个 7 只计一次；已有失败输入 78 会包含 77。

题目 prompt（点击展开）

def fizz_buzz(n: int):
    """Return the number of times the digit 7 appears in integers less than n which are divisible by 11 or 13.
    >>> fizz_buzz(50)
    0
    >>> fizz_buzz(78)
    2
    >>> fizz_buzz(79)
    3
    """

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 135 字符（不是 token 数）

    count = 0
    for i in range(1, n):
        if i % 11 == 0 or i % 13 == 0:
            count += str(i).count('7')
    return count
完整被评分代码 solution

def fizz_buzz(n: int):
    """Return the number of times the digit 7 appears in integers less than n which are divisible by 11 or 13.
    >>> fizz_buzz(50)
    0
    >>> fizz_buzz(78)
    2
    >>> fizz_buzz(79)
    3
    """
    count = 0
    for i in range(1, n):
        if i % 11 == 0 or i % 13 == 0:
            count += str(i).count('7')
    return count

def fizz_buzz(n: int):
    """Return the number of times the digit 7 appears in integers less than n which are divisible by 11 or 13.
    >>> fizz_buzz(50)
    0
    >>> fizz_buzz(78)
    2
    >>> fizz_buzz(79)
    3
    """
    count = 0
    for i in range(1, n):
        if i % 11 == 0 or i % 13 == 0:
            if '7' in str(i):
                count += 1
    return count

HumanEval/49 · 退化 算法逻辑

入口函数：modp

人工静态审阅：SFT 用 pow(2,n,p)。DPO 奇数分支把乘 2 写成乘 modp(2,p)，相当于乘 4；已有失败输入 (3,5)，并且递归实现更复杂。

题目 prompt（点击展开）

def modp(n: int, p: int):
    """Return 2^n modulo p (be aware of numerics).
    >>> modp(3, 5)
    3
    >>> modp(1101, 101)
    2
    >>> modp(0, 101)
    1
    >>> modp(3, 11)
    8
    >>> modp(100, 101)
    1
    """

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 24 字符（不是 token 数）

    return pow(2, n, p)
完整被评分代码 solution

def modp(n: int, p: int):
    """Return 2^n modulo p (be aware of numerics).
    >>> modp(3, 5)
    3
    >>> modp(1101, 101)
    2
    >>> modp(0, 101)
    1
    >>> modp(3, 11)
    8
    >>> modp(100, 101)
    1
    """
    return pow(2, n, p)

        return modp(n // 2, p) ** 2 % p
    else:
        return (modp(n - 1, p) * modp(2, p)) % p
完整被评分代码 solution

def modp(n: int, p: int):
    """Return 2^n modulo p (be aware of numerics).
    >>> modp(3, 5)
    3
    >>> modp(1101, 101)
    2
    >>> modp(0, 101)
    1
    >>> modp(3, 11)
    8
    >>> modp(100, 101)
    1
    """
    if n == 0:
        return 1
    elif n % 2 == 0:
        return modp(n // 2, p) ** 2 % p
    else:
        return (modp(n - 1, p) * modp(2, p)) % p

def is_happy(s):
    """You are given a string s.
    Your task is to check if the string is happy or not.
    A string is happy if its length is at least 3 and every 3 consecutive letters are distinct
    For example:
    is_happy(a) => False
    is_happy(aa) => False
    is_happy(abcd) => True
    is_happy(aabb) => False
    is_happy(adb) => True
    is_happy(xyy) => False
    """
    if len(s) < 3:
        return False
    for i in range(len(s) - 2):
        if s[i] == s[i + 1] or s[i] == s[i + 2] or s[i + 1] == s[i + 2]:
            return False
    return True

def solve(N):
    """Given a positive integer N, return the total sum of its digits in binary.

        For N = 1000, the sum of digits will be 1 the output should be "1".
        For N = 150, the sum of digits will be 6 the output should be "110".
        For N = 147, the sum of digits will be 12 the output should be "1100".
    
    Variables:
        @N integer
             Constraints: 0 ≤ N ≤ 10000.
    Output:
         a string of binary number

def solve(N):
    """Given a positive integer N, return the total sum of its digits in binary.
    
    Example
        For N = 1000, the sum of digits will be 1 the output should be "1".
        For N = 150, the sum of digits will be 6 the output should be "110".
        For N = 147, the sum of digits will be 12 the output should be "1100".
    
    Variables:
        @N integer
             Constraints: 0 ≤ N ≤ 10000.
    Output:
         a string of binary number
    """
    binary_sum = 0
    while N > 0:
        binary_sum += N % 2
        N //= 2
    return bin(binary_sum)[2:]

HumanEval/87 · 退化 算法逻辑

入口函数：get_row

人工静态审阅：SFT 按行升序、列降序排序原坐标。DPO 只按行排序，再用结果序号覆盖原行号，并用长度为 2 的坐标元组反算列号，破坏了坐标及顺序。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
def get_row(lst, x):
    """
    You are given a 2 dimensional data, as a nested lists,
    which is similar to matrix, however, unlike matrices,
    each row may contain a different number of columns.
    Given lst, and integer x, find integers x in the list,
    and return list of tuples, [(x1, y1), (x2, y2) ...] such that
    each tuple is a coordinate - (row, columns), starting with 0.
    Sort coordinates initially by rows in ascending order.
    Also, sort coordinates of the row by columns in descending order.
    
    Examples:
    get_row([
      [1,2,3,4,5,6],
      [1,2,3,4,1,6],
      [1,2,3,4,5,1]
    ], 1) == [(0, 0), (1, 4), (1, 0), (2, 5), (2, 0)]
    get_row([], 1) == []
    get_row([[], [1], [1, 2, 3]], 3) == [(2, 2)]
    """

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 214 字符（不是 token 数）

    result = []
    for i, row in enumerate(lst):
        for j, col in enumerate(row):
            if col == x:
                result.append((i, j))
    result.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], -x[1]))
    return result
完整被评分代码 solution
def get_row(lst, x):
    """
    You are given a 2 dimensional data, as a nested lists,
    which is similar to matrix, however, unlike matrices,
    each row may contain a different number of columns.
    Given lst, and integer x, find integers x in the list,
    and return list of tuples, [(x1, y1), (x2, y2) ...] such that
    each tuple is a coordinate - (row, columns), starting with 0.
    Sort coordinates initially by rows in ascending order.
    Also, sort coordinates of the row by columns in descending order.
    
    Examples:
    get_row([
      [1,2,3,4,5,6],
      [1,2,3,4,1,6],
      [1,2,3,4,5,1]
    ], 1) == [(0, 0), (1, 4), (1, 0), (2, 5), (2, 0)]
    get_row([], 1) == []
    get_row([[], [1], [1, 2, 3]], 3) == [(2, 2)]
    """
    result = []
    for i, row in enumerate(lst):
        for j, col in enumerate(row):
            if col == x:
                result.append((i, j))
    result.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], -x[1]))
    return result

def get_row(lst, x):
    """
    You are given a 2 dimensional data, as a nested lists,
    which is similar to matrix, however, unlike matrices,
    each row may contain a different number of columns.
    Given lst, and integer x, find integers x in the list,
    and return list of tuples, [(x1, y1), (x2, y2) ...] such that
    each tuple is a coordinate - (row, columns), starting with 0.
    Sort coordinates initially by rows in ascending order.
    Also, sort coordinates of the row by columns in descending order.
    
    Examples:
    get_row([
      [1,2,3,4,5,6],
      [1,2,3,4,1,6],
      [1,2,3,4,5,1]
    ], 1) == [(0, 0), (1, 4), (1, 0), (2, 5), (2, 0)]
    get_row([], 1) == []
    get_row([[], [1], [1, 2, 3]], 3) == [(2, 2)]

        for j, value in enumerate(row):
            if value == x:
                result.append((i, j))
    result.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    for i, row in enumerate(result):
        result[i] = (i, len(row) - 1 - row[1])
    return result

def even_odd_count(num):
    """Given an integer. return a tuple that has the number of even and odd digits respectively.

        even_odd_count(-12) ==> (1, 1)

完整被评分代码 solution
def even_odd_count(num):
    """Given an integer. return a tuple that has the number of even and odd digits respectively.

     Example:
        even_odd_count(-12) ==> (1, 1)
        even_odd_count(123) ==> (1, 2)
    """
    num = str(abs(num))
    even_count = 0
    odd_count = 0
    for digit in num:
        if int(digit) % 2 == 0:
            even_count += 1
        else:
            odd_count += 1
    return (even_count, odd_count)

完整被评分代码 solution
def even_odd_count(num):
    """Given an integer. return a tuple that has the number of even and odd digits respectively.

     Example:
        even_odd_count(-12) ==> (1, 1)
        even_odd_count(123) ==> (1, 2)
    """
    num = abs(num)
    even_count = 0
    odd_count = 0
    
    while num > 0:
        digit = num % 10
        if digit % 2 == 0:
            even_count += 1
        else:
            odd_count += 1
        num //= 10
    
    return (even_count, odd_count)

Mbpp/71 · 退化 算法逻辑

入口函数：comb_sort

人工静态审阅：DPO 在 gap 仍大于 1 时也把 sorted 设为 True，无交换即可提前退出。已有输入 [5,15,37,25,79] 在 gap=3 的一轮无交换后仍未排序完成。base=fail、plus=pass 仍按项目约定算失败。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a function to sort a list of elements.
assert comb_sort([5, 15, 37, 25, 79]) == [5, 15, 25, 37, 79]
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 629 字符（不是 token 数）

def comb_sort(arr):
    """
    Sorts a list of elements using the comb sort algorithm.
    
    Parameters:
    arr (list): The list of elements to be sorted.
    
    Returns:
    list: The sorted list of elements.
    """
    gap = len(arr)
    shrink_factor = 1.3
    sorted = False

    while not sorted:
        gap = int(gap / shrink_factor)
        if gap <= 1:
            gap = 1
            sorted = True

        i = 0
        while i + gap < len(arr):
            if arr[i] > arr[i + gap]:
                arr[i], arr[i + gap] = arr[i + gap], arr[i]
                sorted = False
            i += 1

    return arr
完整被评分代码 solution

def is_Diff(n):
    """
    Determine if a given number is divisible by 11.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): The number to check.
    
    Returns:
    bool: True if the number is divisible by 11, False otherwise.
    """
    str_n = str(n)
    sum_even = 0
    sum_odd = 0
    
    for i, digit in enumerate(str_n):
        if i % 2 == 0:
            sum_even += int(digit)
        else:
            sum_odd += int(digit)
    
    return abs(sum_even - sum_odd) == 0

Mbpp/84 · 退化 算法逻辑

入口函数：sequence

人工静态审阅：DPO 改动 Newman-Conway 的初值和递推式：n=2 返回 2，递推也不再是原 DP 的两项索引形式。已有 n=10 失败；可能还有递归性能问题，但未重跑确认。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a function to find the nth number in the newman conway sequence.
assert sequence(10) == 6
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 582 字符（不是 token 数）

def sequence(n):
    """
    Returns the nth number in the newman conway sequence.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): The position in the sequence to find.
    
    Returns:
    int: The nth number in the newman conway sequence.
    """
    def newman_conway(n):
        if n <= 0:
            return 0
        elif n == 1:
            return 1
        else:
            dp = [0] * (n + 1)
            dp[1] = 1
            dp[2] = 1
            for i in range(3, n + 1):
                dp[i] = dp[dp[i - 1]] + dp[i - dp[i - 1]]
            return dp[n]
    return newman_conway(n)
完整被评分代码 solution

def sequence(n):
    """
    Returns the nth number in the newman conway sequence.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): The position in the sequence to find.
    
    Returns:
    int: The nth number in the newman conway sequence.
    """
    def newman_conway(n):
        if n <= 0:
            return 0
        elif n == 1:
            return 1
        else:
            dp = [0] * (n + 1)
            dp[1] = 1
            dp[2] = 1
            for i in range(3, n + 1):

def sequence(n):
    """
    Returns the nth number in the newman conway sequence.
    
    The newman conway sequence is defined as follows:
    - newman_conway(1) = 1
    - newman_conway(i) = newman_conway(newman_conway(i-1)) + newman_conway(i-2) for i > 1
    """
    if n == 1:
        return 1
    elif n == 2:
        return 2
    else:
        def newman_conway_helper(i):
            if i == 1:
                return 1
            elif i == 2:
                return 2
            else:
                return newman_conway_helper(newman_conway_helper(i-1)) + newman_conway_helper(i-2)
        return newman_conway_helper(n)

def next_smallest_palindrome(n):
    """
    Returns the next smallest palindrome greater than the given integer n.
    
    :param n: Integer, the starting number to find the next palindrome for.
    :return: Integer, the next smallest palindrome greater than n.
    """
    n += 1
    while not is_palindrome(n):
        n += 1
    return n

def is_palindrome(num):
    """
    Checks if a given number is a palindrome.
    
    :param num: Integer, the number to check.
    :return: Boolean, True if the number is a palindrome, False otherwise.
    """
    return str(num) == str(num)[::-1]

def next_smallest_palindrome(n):
    """
    Finds the next smallest palindrome greater than the given integer n.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): The integer to find the next smallest palindrome for.
    
    Returns:
    int: The next smallest palindrome integer.
    """
    # Convert the number to a string to easily manipulate individual digits
    str_n = str(n)
    # If the number is a single digit, the next palindrome is the next even digit
    if len(str_n) == 1:
        return int(str_n) + 1 if int(str_n) % 2 == 0 else int(str_n) + 2
    # If the number has an odd number of digits, find the middle digit
    if len(str_n) % 2 == 1:
        mid = len(str_n) // 2
        # If the middle digit is '0', increment the middle digit and all digits to the right
        if str_n[mid] == '0':
            return int(str_n[:mid] + '1' + str_n[mid+1:])
        # If the middle digit is not '0', increment it and all digits to the right
        return int(str_n[:mid] + str(int(str_n[mid]) + 1) + str_n[mid+1:])
    # If the number has an even number of digits, find the two middle digits
    else:
        mid1 = len(str_n) // 2 - 1
        mid2 = len(str_n) // 2
        # If both middle digits are '0', increment the middle digits and all digits to the right
        if str_n[mid1] == '0' and str_n[mid2] == '0':
            return int(str_n[:mid1] + '1' + '1' + str_n[mid1+2:])
        # If only the first middle digit is '0', increment the first middle digit and all digits to the right
        elif str_n[mid1] == '0':
            return int(str_n[:mid1] + '1' + str_n[mid1+1:])
        # If only the second middle digit is '0', increment the second middle digit and all digits to the right
        elif str_n[mid2] == '0':
            return int(str_n[:mid2] + str(int(str_n[mid2]) + 1) + str_n[mid2+1:])
        # If both

Mbpp/109 · 退化 题意与规则

入口函数：odd_Equivalent

人工静态审阅：DPO 把 SFT 的左旋改为右旋；公开题干没有写清方向，但已有新增测试使用了有方向差异的输入。应记录为方向与评测约定不符，不把题干当作毫无歧义。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a python function to find the number of numbers with an odd value when rotating a binary string the given number of times.
assert odd_Equivalent("011001",6) == 3
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 800 字符（不是 token 数）

def odd_Equivalent(binary_string, rotations):
    """
    Calculate the number of numbers with an odd value when rotating a binary string the given number of times.

    :param binary_string: A string representing a binary number.
    :param rotations: An integer representing the number of times the binary string is rotated.
    :return: An integer representing the count of numbers with an odd value after rotations.
    """
    def rotate_left(s, k):
        return s[k:] + s[:k]

    def count_odd_numbers(binary_string, rotations):
        count = 0
        for _ in range(rotations):
            binary_string = rotate_left(binary_string, 1)
            if int(binary_string, 2) % 2 != 0:
                count += 1
        return count

    return count_odd_numbers(binary_string, rotations)
完整被评分代码 solution

Mbpp/120 · 退化 顶层断言

入口函数：max_product_tuple

人工静态审阅：目标函数主体与 SFT 数学上等价。DPO 额外生成顶层 assert，把 [(10,20),(15,5),(7,14)] 的最大乘积写成 210，实际为 200。导入时错误断言会阻止测试执行；这是静态算术判断，未重新执行该程序。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a function to find the maximum absolute product between numbers in pairs of tuples within a given list.
assert max_product_tuple([(2, 7), (2, 6), (1, 8), (4, 9)] )==36
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 493 字符（不是 token 数）

def max_product_tuple(tuples_list):
    """
    Finds the maximum absolute product between numbers in pairs of tuples within a given list.

    :param tuples_list: List of tuples, where each tuple contains two integers.
    :return: The maximum absolute product of any pair of numbers in the tuples.
    """
    max_product = float('-inf')
    for t1, t2 in tuples_list:
        product = abs(t1 * t2)
        if product > max_product:
            max_product = product
    return max_product

def max_product_tuple(tuples_list):
    """
    Finds the maximum absolute product between numbers in pairs of tuples within a given list.

    :param tuples_list: List of tuples, where each tuple contains two integers.
    :return: The maximum absolute product found among all pairs in the list.
    """
    max_product = float('-inf')
    for a, b in tuples_list:
        current_product = abs(a) * abs(b)
        if current_product > max_product:
            max_product = current_product
    return max_product

# Test cases

assert max_product_tuple([(10, 20), (15, 5), (7, 14)]) == 210
assert max_product_tuple([(3, -2), (4, -5), (6, 7)]) == 42

Mbpp/142 · 退化 边界与返回约定

入口函数：count_samepair

人工静态审阅：SFT 用 zip 遍历公共长度。DPO 按 list1 全长索引其他列表，且长度检查使用链式 !=，既可能误拒绝不同长度，也可能越界；已有长度为 6、5、8 的失败输入。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a function to count number items that are identical in the same position of three given lists.
assert count_samepair([1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8],[2,2,3,1,2,6,7,9],[2,1,3,1,2,6,7,9])==3
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 481 字符（不是 token 数）

def count_samepair(list1, list2, list3):
    """
    Counts the number of items that are identical in the same position of three given lists.

    Parameters:
    list1 (list): The first list of integers.
    list2 (list): The second list of integers.
    list3 (list): The third list of integers.

    Returns:
    int: The count of items that are identical in the same position of the three lists.
    """
    return sum(1 for a, b, c in zip(list1, list2, list3) if a == b == c)
完整被评分代码 solution
"""
Write a function to count number items that are identical in the same position of three given lists.
assert count_samepair([1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8],[2,2,3,1,2,6,7,9],[2,1,3,1,2,6,7,9])==3
"""
def count_samepair(list1, list2, list3):
    """
    Counts the number of items that are identical in the same position of three given lists.

    Parameters:
    list1 (list): The first list of integers.
    list2 (list): The second list of integers.
    list3 (list): The third list of integers.

    Returns:
    int: The count of items that are identical in the same position of the three lists.
    """
    return sum(1 for a, b, c in zip(list1, list2, list3) if a == b == c)

def count_samepair(list1, list2, list3):
    """
    Counts the number of items that are identical in the same position of three given lists.

    Parameters:
    list1 (list): The first list of integers.
    list2 (list): The second list of integers.
    list3 (list): The third list of integers.

    Returns:
    int: The count of items that are identical in the same position of the three lists.
    """
    if len(list1) != len(list2) != len(list3):
        return 0  # If the lists are of different lengths, there can't be any matching pairs.
    
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(list1)):
        if list1[i] == list2[i] == list3[i]:
            count += 1
            
    return count

Mbpp/160 · 退化 算法逻辑

入口函数：find_solution

人工静态审阅：DPO 改成不正确的欧几里得式求解：用 n 替代系数参与递推，不能保证 ax+by=n；已有 (2,3,7) 失败，后续 x<0 分支还可能用已变为 0 的 b 作除数。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a function that returns integers x and y that satisfy ax + by = n as a tuple, or return None if no solution exists.
assert find_solution(2, 3, 7) == (2, 1)
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 411 字符（不是 token 数）

def find_solution(a, b, n):
    """
    Returns a tuple (x, y) such that ax + by = n, or None if no such integers exist.
    
    :param a: Integer coefficient of x
    :param b: Integer coefficient of y
    :param n: Integer target sum
    :return: Tuple (x, y) or None
    """
    for x in range(n // a + 1):
        y = (n - a * x) // b
        if a * x + b * y == n:
            return x, y
    return None

def find_solution(a, b, n):
    """
    Finds integers x and y such that ax + by = n.
    
    Parameters:
    a (int): The coefficient of x.
    b (int): The coefficient of y.
    n (int): The target sum.
    
    Returns:
    tuple: A tuple (x, y) if a solution exists, otherwise None.
    """
    # Check if the GCD of a and b divides n
    if (n % gcd(a, b)) != 0:
        return None
    
    # Initialize x and y
    x, y = 0, 1
    
    # Extended Euclidean Algorithm to find x and y
    while b != 0:
        quotient = n // b
        x, y = y, x - quotient * y
        n, b = b, n % b
    
    # Ensure the solution is the smallest non-negative solution
    if x < 0:
        x += n // b
    
    return (x, y)

def gcd(x, y):
    """
    Computes the greatest common divisor of x and y using the Euclidean algorithm.
    
    Parameters:
    x (int): The first number.
    y (int): The second number.
    
    Returns:
    int: The greatest common divisor of x and y.
    """
    while y:
        x, y = y, x % y
    return x

入口函数：count_char_position

题目 prompt（点击展开）

Write a function to count the number of characters in a string that occur at the same position in the string as in the English alphabet (case insensitive).
assert count_char_position("xbcefg") == 2
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 495 字符（不是 token 数）

def count_char_position(s):
    """
    Counts the number of characters in the string that occur at the same position
    in the string as in the English alphabet (case insensitive).

    :param s: The input string to be analyzed.
    :return: The count of characters at the same position in the string as in the English alphabet.
    """
    lower_s = s.lower()
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(lower_s)):
        if lower_s[i] == chr(ord('a') + i):
            count += 1
    return count

def count_char_position(s):
    """
    Counts the number of characters in the string that occur at the same position
    in the string as in the English alphabet (case insensitive).

    :param s: The input string to be analyzed.
    :return: The count of characters at the same position as in the English alphabet.

            count += 1
    return count

def validate(number):
    """
    Check if the frequency of each digit in the number is less than or equal to the digit itself.
    
    :param number: Integer to be checked
    :return: True if the condition is met, False otherwise
    """
    number_str = str(number)
    for digit in number_str:
        if int(digit) < number_str.count(digit):
            return False
    return True

    Drops empty items from the given dictionary.

    Parameters:
    d (dict): The dictionary from which to drop empty items.

    Returns:
    dict: A new dictionary with all empty items removed.
    """

Mbpp/592 · 退化 算法逻辑

入口函数：sum_Of_product

人工静态审阅：相邻二项式系数的乘积 comb(n,i)*comb(n,i+1) 被改为 comb(n,i)**2，求的是不同的和；已有 n=3 失败。

"""

from math import comb

def sum_Of_product(n):
    """
    Calculate the sum of the product of consecutive binomial coefficients from 0 to n.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): The upper limit for the binomial coefficients.
    
    Returns:
    int: The sum of the product of consecutive binomial coefficients.
    """
    total_sum = 0
    for i in range(n + 1):
        total_sum += comb(n, i) * comb(n, i + 1)
    return total_sum

def first_repeated_char(s):
    """
    Returns the first character that appears more than once in the input string.
    
    :param s: The input string to search for the first repeated character.
    :return: The first repeated character, or None if no such character exists.
    """
    char_count = {}
    for char in s:
        if char in char_count:
            return char
        char_count[char] = 1
    return None

Mbpp/608 · 退化 算法逻辑

入口函数：bell_Number

人工静态审阅：SFT 使用 Bell 三角形。DPO 用前面 bell 值的直接求和，缺少 Bell 数所需的组合系数或正确三角递推；已有 n=3 失败。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a python function to find nth bell number.
assert bell_Number(2) == 2
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 461 字符（不是 token 数）

def bell_Number(n):
    """
    Calculate the nth Bell number using dynamic programming.

    Parameters:
    n (int): The order of the Bell number to compute.

    Returns:
    int: The nth Bell number.
    """
    bell = [[0 for _ in range(n+1)] for _ in range(n+1)]
    bell[0][0] = 1
    for i in range(1, n+1):
        bell[i][0] = bell[i-1][i-1]
        for j in range(1, i+1):
            bell[i][j] = bell[i-1][j-1] + bell[i][j-1]
    return bell[n][0]
完整被评分代码 solution

def bell_Number(n):
    """
    Calculate the nth Bell number using dynamic programming.

    Returns:
    int: The nth Bell number.

def bell_Number(n):
    """
    Calculate the nth Bell number using dynamic programming.

    Parameters:
    n (int): The order of the Bell number to compute.

    Returns:
    int: The nth Bell number.
    """
    if n == 0:
        return 1
    bell = [0] * (n + 1)
    bell[0] = 1
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        bell[i] = sum(bell[j] for j in range(i))
    return bell[n]

"""

    """
    Returns the maximum value in the n'th column of the given matrix.

    :param matrix: List of lists representing the matrix.
    :param n: Integer representing the column index.
    :return: Integer, the maximum value in the n'th column.
    """
    return max([row[n] for row in matrix])

def max_of_nth(matrix, n):
    """
    Returns the maximum value in the n'th column of the given matrix.
    
    :param matrix: List of lists, where each sublist represents a row in the matrix.
    :param n: Integer, the index of the column to find the maximum value for.
    :return: Integer, the maximum value in the n'th column.
    """
    if not matrix or n < 0 or n >= len(matrix[0]):
        return None  # Return None if the matrix is empty or n is out of range
    
    max_value = float('-inf')
    for row in matrix:
        if row[n] > max_value:
            max_value = row[n]
    
    return max_value

Mbpp/632 · 退化 入口函数名

入口函数：move_zero

人工静态审阅：题目入口 move_zero 被改为 move_zeroes；此外算法改为原地修改列表。当前最直接可核实的失败线索是完整模块缺少指定入口，不能据此先断言原地修改是本次失败原因。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a python function to move all zeroes to the end of the given list.
assert move_zero([1,0,2,0,3,4]) == [1,2,3,4,0,0]
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 464 字符（不是 token 数）

def move_zero(lst):
    """
    Moves all zeroes in the list to the end while maintaining the order of non-zero elements.
    
    Parameters:
    lst (list): The list of integers to be processed.
    
    Returns:
    list: A new list with zeroes moved to the end.
    """
    non_zero = [x for x in lst if x != 0]
    zero = [x for x in lst if x == 0]
    return non_zero + zero

# Test the function
print(move_zero([1,0,2,0,3,4]))  # Output: [1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 0]

def move_zero(lst):
    """
    Moves all zeroes in the list to the end while maintaining the order of non-zero elements.
    
    Parameters:
    lst (list): The list of integers to be processed.
    
    Returns:
    list: A new list with zeroes moved to the end.
    """

def move_zeroes(nums):
    """
    Moves all zeroes in the list to the end while maintaining the order of other elements.
    
    :param nums: List of integers
    :return: List of integers with zeroes moved to the end
    """
    non_zero_index = 0
    for i in range(len(nums)):
        if nums[i] != 0:
            nums[non_zero_index], nums[i] = nums[i], nums[non_zero_index]
            non_zero_index += 1
    return nums

"""

def pair_xor_Sum(arr, n):
    """
    Calculate the sum of XOR of all pairs in the given list.

    :param arr: List of integers
    :param n: Number of elements in the list
    :return: Sum of XOR of all pairs
    """
    xor_sum = 0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i+1, n):
            xor_sum += arr[i] ^ arr[j]
    return xor_sum

Mbpp/767 · 退化 算法逻辑

入口函数：get_pairs_count

人工静态审阅：DPO 使用 set 只记录是否出现，丢失重复元素次数；四个 1、目标和为 2 时应有 6 对，代码逻辑只会计 3 对。已有该输入失败。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a python function to count the number of pairs whose sum is equal to ‘sum’. The funtion gets as input a list of numbers and the sum,
assert get_pairs_count([1,1,1,1],2) == 6
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 507 字符（不是 token 数）

def get_pairs_count(lst, sum):
    """
    Counts the number of pairs in the list whose sum is equal to the given sum.

    :param lst: List of integers.
    :param sum: Integer representing the target sum.
    :return: Integer count of pairs with the given sum.
    """
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(lst)):
        for j in range(i+1, len(lst)):
            if lst[i] + lst[j] == sum:
                count += 1
    return count

# Test the function
print(get_pairs_count([1,1,1,1],2))  # Output: 6

def get_pairs_count(nums, target):
    """
    Counts the number of pairs in the list that add up to the target sum.

    :param nums: List of integers.
    :param target: Integer representing the target sum.
    :return: Integer count of pairs that add up to the target sum.
    """
    count = 0
    num_set = set()
    for num in nums:
        complement = target - num
        if complement in num_set:
            count += 1
        num_set.add(num)
    return count

# Example usage:
print(get_pairs_count([1, 1, 1, 1], 2))  # Output: 6

    Removes all nested tuples from the given tuple.

    Parameters:
    t (tuple): The input tuple from which nested tuples need to be removed.

    Returns:
    tuple: A new tuple with all nested tuples removed.
    """

def remove_nested(t):
    """
    Removes all nested tuples from the given tuple.

    Parameters:
    t (tuple): The input tuple from which nested tuples need to be removed.

    Returns:
    tuple: A new tuple with all nested tuples removed.
    """
    result = []
    for item in t:
        if isinstance(item, tuple):
            result.extend(remove_nested(item))
        else:
            result.append(item)
    return tuple(result)

Mbpp/808 · 退化 题意与规则

入口函数：check_K

人工静态审阅：题目要求 k 是否为输入 tuple 的元素。DPO 把它理解为 tuple 列表，逐个执行 k in t；原始数字 tuple 会触发不可迭代元素错误，另有嵌套结构的新增测试失败。

"""
Write a function to check if the given tuples contain the k or not.
assert check_K((10, 4, 5, 6, 8), 6) == True
"""

def check_K(tuples, k):
    """
    Check if the given tuples contain the k or not.

    Parameters:
    tuples (tuple): A tuple of integers.
    k (int): The integer to check for in the tuple.

    Returns:
    bool: True if k is found in the tuple, False otherwise.
    """
    return k in tuples

def check_K(tuples_list, k):
    """
    Check if any tuple in the list contains the integer k.

    :param tuples_list: List of tuples to be checked.
    :param k: Integer to be checked for presence in the tuples.
    :return: True if at least one tuple contains k, False otherwise.
    """
    for t in tuples_list:
        if k in t:
            return True
    return False

from typing import List

    False

    True

完整被评分代码 solution
from typing import List


def has_close_elements(numbers: List[float], threshold: float) -> bool:
    """ Check if in given list of numbers, are any two numbers closer to each other than
    given threshold.
    >>> has_close_elements([1.0, 2.0, 3.0], 0.5)
    False
    >>> has_close_elements([1.0, 2.8, 3.0, 4.0, 5.0, 2.0], 0.3)
    True
    """
    for i in range(len(numbers)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(numbers)):
            if abs(numbers[i] - numbers[j]) < threshold:
                return True
    return False


def has_close_elements_2(numbers: List[float], threshold: float) -> bool:

    True

        for j in range(i + 1, len(numbers)):
            if abs(numbers[i] - numbers[j]) < threshold:
                return True
    return False

入口函数：mean_absolute_deviation

人工静态审阅：SFT 有语法失败，DPO 的平均绝对偏差实现可解析并通过测试；DPO 仍追加无关统计函数，末尾 z_score 留下单独 std 表达式，说明通过并不代表消除了无关续写。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
from typing import List


def mean_absolute_deviation(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate Mean Absolute Deviation
    around the mean of this dataset.
    Mean Absolute Deviation is the average absolute difference between each
    element and a centerpoint (mean in this case):
    MAD = average | x - x_mean |
    >>> mean_absolute_deviation([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    1.0
    """

LoRA SFT · base=fail / plus=fail

原始 completion · 1768 字符（不是 token 数）

    mean = sum(numbers) / len(numbers)
    return sum(abs(x - mean) for x in numbers) / len(numbers)


def mean_absolute_percentage_error(actual: List[float], predicted: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate Mean Absolute Percentage Error
    around the mean of this dataset.
    Mean Absolute Percentage Error is the average absolute difference between each
    element and a centerpoint (mean in this case):
    MAPE = average | (x - x_mean) / x_mean |
    >>> mean_absolute_percentage_error([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0], [1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    0.0
    """
    mean = sum(actual) / len(actual)
    return sum(abs((x - mean) / mean) for x in actual) / len(actual)


def mean_squared_error(actual: List[float], predicted: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate Mean Squared Error
    around the mean of this dataset.
    Mean Squared Error is the average squared difference between each
    element and a centerpoint (mean in this case):
    MSE = average (x - x_mean)^2
    >>> mean_squared_error([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0], [1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    0.0
    """
    mean = sum(actual) / len(actual)
    return sum((x - mean) ** 2 for x in actual) / len(actual)

完整被评分代码 solution
from typing import List


def mean_absolute_deviation(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate Mean Absolute Deviation
    around the mean of this dataset.
    Mean Absolute Deviation is the average absolute difference between each
    element and a centerpoint (mean in this case):
    MAD = average | x - x_mean |
    >>> mean_absolute_deviation([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    1.0
    """
    mean = sum(numbers) / len(numbers)
    return sum(abs(x - mean) for x in numbers) / len(numbers)


def mean_absolute_percentage_error(actual: List[float], predicted: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate Mean Absolute Percentage Error
    around the mean of this dataset.
    Mean Absolute Percentage Error is the average absolute difference between each
    element and a centerpoint (mean in this case):
    MAPE = average | (x - x_mean) / x_mean |
    >>> mean_absolute_percentage_error([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0], [1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    0.0
    """
    mean = sum(actual) / len(actual)
    return sum(abs((x - mean) / mean) for x in actual) / len(actual)


def mean_squared_error(actual: List[float], predicted: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate Mean Squared Error
    around the mean of this dataset.
    Mean Squared Error is the average squared difference between each
    element and a centerpoint (mean in this case):
    MSE = average (x - x_mean)^2
    >>> mean_squared_error([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0], [1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    0.0
    """
    mean = sum(actual) / len(actual)
    return sum((x - mean) ** 2 for x in actual) / len(actual)

def mean(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate the mean of this dataset.
    >>> mean([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    2.5
    """
    return sum(numbers) / len(numbers)


def median(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate the median of this dataset.
    >>> median([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    2.5
    """
    sorted_numbers = sorted(numbers)
    n = len(sorted_numbers)
    mid = n // 2
    if n % 2 == 0:
        return (sorted_numbers[mid - 1] + sorted_numbers[mid]) / 2
    else:
        return sorted_numbers[mid]


def variance(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate the variance of this dataset.
    Variance is the average of the squared differences from the mean.
    >>> variance([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    1.25
    """
    mean_value = mean(numbers)
    return sum((x - mean_value) ** 2 for x in numbers) / len(numbers)


def standard_deviation(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate the standard deviation of this dataset.
    Standard deviation is the square root of the variance.
    >>> standard_deviation([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    1.1180339887498948
    """
    return variance(numbers) ** 0.5


def z_score(numbers: List[float]) -> List[float]:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate the z-score of each number.
    Z-score is the number of standard deviations from the mean.
    >>> z_score([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    [0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5]
    """
    mean_value = mean(numbers)
    std

from typing import List


def mean_absolute_deviation(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate Mean Absolute Deviation
    around the mean of this dataset.
    Mean Absolute Deviation is the average absolute difference between each
    element and a centerpoint (mean in this case):
    MAD = average | x - x_mean |
    >>> mean_absolute_deviation([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    1.0
    """
    mean = sum(numbers) / len(numbers)
    return sum(abs(x - mean) for x in numbers) / len(numbers)


def mean(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate the mean of this dataset.
    >>> mean([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    2.5
    """
    return sum(numbers) / len(numbers)


def median(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate the median of this dataset.
    >>> median([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    2.5
    """
    sorted_numbers = sorted(numbers)
    n = len(sorted_numbers)
    mid = n // 2
    if n % 2 == 0:
        return (sorted_numbers[mid - 1] + sorted_numbers[mid]) / 2
    else:
        return sorted_numbers[mid]


def variance(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate the variance of this dataset.
    Variance is the average of the squared differences from the mean.
    >>> variance([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    1.25
    """
    mean_value = mean(numbers)
    return sum((x - mean_value) ** 2 for x in numbers) / len(numbers)


def standard_deviation(numbers: List[float]) -> float:
    """ For a given list of input numbers, calculate the standard deviation of this dataset.
    Standard deviation is the square root of the variance.
    >>> standard_deviation([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0])
    1.1180339887498948
    """
    return variance(numbers) ** 0.5

from typing import List


def intersperse(numbers: List[int], delimeter: int) -> List[int]:
    """ Insert a number 'delimeter' between every two consecutive elements of input list `numbers'
    >>> intersperse([], 4)
    []
    >>> intersperse([1, 2, 3], 4)
    [1, 4, 2, 4, 3]

        return []
    return [numbers[0]] + [delimeter] * (len(numbers) - 1) + [numbers[-1]]

from typing import List


def intersperse(numbers: List[int], delimeter: int) -> List[int]:

    return result

from typing import List, Tuple


def sum_product(numbers: List[int]) -> Tuple[int, int]:
    """ For a given list of integers, return a tuple consisting of a sum and a product of all the integers in a list.
    Empty sum should be equal to 0 and empty product should be equal to 1.
    >>> sum_product([])
    (0, 1)
    >>> sum_product([1, 2, 3, 4])
    (10, 24)
    """
    if not numbers:
        return 0, 1
    return sum(numbers), 1 if not numbers else numbers[0] * sum_product(numbers[1:])[0]

from typing import List, Tuple


def sum_product(numbers: List[int]) -> Tuple[int, int]:
    """ For a given list of integers, return a tuple consisting of a sum and a product of all the integers in a list.
    Empty sum should be equal to 0 and empty product should be equal to 1.

    (0, 1)
    >>> sum_product([1, 2, 3, 4])
    (10, 24)

    total_sum = 0

        total_sum += number
        total_product *= number
    return total_sum, total_product

def is_prime(n):
    """Return true if a given number is prime, and false otherwise.
    >>> is_prime(6)
    False
    >>> is_prime(101)
    True
    >>> is_prime(11)
    True
    >>> is_prime(13441)
    True
    >>> is_prime(61)
    True
    >>> is_prime(4)
    False
    >>> is_prime(1)
    False
    """
    if n == 1:
        return False
    if n == 2:
        return True
    if n % 2 == 0:
        return False
    for i in range(3, int(n**0.5) + 1, 2):
        if n % i == 0:
            return False
    return True

def is_prime(n):
    """Return true if a given number is prime, and false otherwise.
    >>> is_prime(6)
    False
    >>> is_prime(101)
    True
    >>> is_prime(11)
    True
    >>> is_prime(13441)
    True
    >>> is_prime(61)
    True
    >>> is_prime(4)
    False
    >>> is_prime(1)
    False
    """
    if n <= 1:
        return False
    if n <= 3:
        return True
    if n % 2 == 0 or n % 3 == 0:
        return False
    i = 5
    while i * i <= n:
        if n % i == 0 or n % (i + 2) == 0:
            return False
        i += 6
    return True

    """This function takes a list l and returns a list l' such that
    l' is identical to l in the odd indicies, while its values at the even indicies are equal

    [1, 2, 3]
    >>> sort_even([5, 6, 3, 4])
    [3, 6, 5, 4]
    """
    even_indices = [l[i] for i in range(0, len(l), 2)]
    even_indices.sort()
    result = [l[i] if i % 2 != 0 else even_indices[i // 2] for i in range(len(l))]
    return result

HumanEval/38 · 改善 算法逻辑

入口函数：decode_cyclic

人工静态审阅：SFT 的 decode 不执行逆旋转；DPO 将每组三字符向右旋转以撤销 encode 的左旋。

题目 prompt（点击展开）

def encode_cyclic(s: str):
    """
    returns encoded string by cycling groups of three characters.
    """
    # split string to groups. Each of length 3.
    groups = [s[(3 * i):min((3 * i + 3), len(s))] for i in range((len(s) + 2) // 3)]
    # cycle elements in each group. Unless group has fewer elements than 3.
    groups = [(group[1:] + group[0]) if len(group) == 3 else group for group in groups]
    return "".join(groups)


def decode_cyclic(s: str):
    """
    takes as input string encoded with encode_cyclic function. Returns decoded string.
    """

    """Check if x is a prime number
    >>> is_prime(2)
    True
    >>> is_prime(4)
    False
    >>> is_prime(17)
    True
    """
    if x <= 1:
        return False
    if x <= 3:
        return True
    if x % 2 == 0 or x % 3 == 0:
        return False
    i = 5
    while i * i <= x:
        if x % i == 0 or x % (i + 2) == 0:
            return False
        i += 6
    return True

入口函数：fib

人工静态审阅：DPO 增加 n=0 边界并采用迭代 Fibonacci；SFT 的无缓存递归还存在明显性能风险。具体失败机制未重跑区分。

def fib(n: int):
    """Return n-th Fibonacci number.
    >>> fib(10)
    55
    >>> fib(1)
    1
    >>> fib(8)
    21
    """

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=fail

        return 1

完整被评分代码 solution

def fib(n: int):
    """Return n-th Fibonacci number.
    >>> fib(10)
    55
    >>> fib(1)
    1
    >>> fib(8)
    21
    """
    if n == 1 or n == 2:
        return 1
    else:
        return fib(n-1) + fib(n-2)

            a, b = b, a + b
        return b
完整被评分代码 solution

def fib(n: int):
    """Return n-th Fibonacci number.
    >>> fib(10)
    55
    >>> fib(1)
    1
    >>> fib(8)
    21
    """
    if n <= 0:
        return 0
    elif n == 1:
        return 1
    else:
        a, b = 0, 1
        for _ in range(2, n + 1):
            a, b = b, a + b
        return b

  }
}

def vowels_count(s):
    """Write a function vowels_count which takes a string representing

def vowels_count(s):
    """Write a function vowels_count which takes a string representing
    a word as input and returns the number of vowels in the string.
    Vowels in this case are 'a', 'e', 'i', 'o', 'u'. Here, 'y' is also a
    vowel, but only when it is at the end of the given word.

    Example:
    >>> vowels_count("abcde")
    2
    >>> vowels_count("ACEDY")
    3
    """
    vowels = {'a', 'e', 'i', 'o', 'u'}
    count = sum(1 for char in s.lower() if char in vowels)
    if s.endswith('y') or s.endswith('Y'):
        count += 1
    return count

        Output: [2, 1]

def search(lst):
    '''
    You are given a non-empty list of positive integers. Return the greatest integer that is greater than 
    zero, and has a frequency greater than or equal to the value of the integer itself. 
    The frequency of an integer is the number of times it appears in the list.
    If no such a value exist, return -1.
    Examples:
        search([4, 1, 2, 2, 3, 1]) == 2
        search([1, 2, 2, 3, 3, 3, 4, 4, 4]) == 3
        search([5, 5, 4, 4, 4]) == -1
    '''
    for i in range(1, max(lst) + 1):
        if lst.count(i) >= i:
            return i
    return -1

def next_smallest(lst):
    """
    You are given a list of integers.
    Write a function next_smallest() that returns the 2nd smallest element of the list.
    Return None if there is no such element.
    
    next_smallest([1, 2, 3, 4, 5]) == 2
    next_smallest([5, 1, 4, 3, 2]) == 2
    next_smallest([]) == None
    next_smallest([1, 1]) == None
    """
    if len(lst) < 2:
        return None
    first, second = float('inf'), float('inf')
    for num in lst:
        if num < first:
            second = first
            first = num
        elif num < second and num != first:
            second = num
    return second

def next_smallest(lst):
    """
    You are given a list of integers.
    Write a function next_smallest() that returns the 2nd smallest element of the list.
    Return None if there is no such element.

    next_smallest([]) == None
    next_smallest([1, 1]) == None

    for num in lst:
        if num < first:
            second = first
            first = num
        elif num < second and num != first:
            second = num

    return second if second != float('inf') else None

HumanEval/109 · 改善 题意与规则

入口函数：move_one_ball

人工静态审阅：SFT 只检查原数组是否已排序；DPO 检查循环移位后能否得到排序数组。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
def move_one_ball(arr):
    """We have an array 'arr' of N integers arr[1], arr[2], ..., arr[N].The
    numbers in the array will be randomly ordered. Your task is to determine if
    it is possible to get an array sorted in non-decreasing order by performing 
    the following operation on the given array:
        You are allowed to perform right shift operation any number of times.
    
    One right shift operation means shifting all elements of the array by one
    position in the right direction. The last element of the array will be moved to
    the starting position in the array i.e. 0th index. 

    If it is possible to obtain the sorted array by performing the above operation
    then return True else return False.
    If the given array is empty then return True.

    Note: The given list is guaranteed to have unique elements.

    For Example:
    
    move_one_ball([3, 4, 5, 1, 2])==>True
    Explanation: By performin 2 right shift operations, non-decreasing order can
                 be achieved for the given array.
    move_one_ball([3, 5, 4, 1, 2])==>False
    Explanation:It is not possible to get non-decreasing order for the given

def move_one_ball(arr):
    """We have an array 'arr' of N integers arr[1], arr[2], ..., arr[N].The
    numbers in the array will be randomly ordered. Your task is to determine if
    it is possible to get an array sorted in non-decreasing order by performing 
    the following operation on the given array:
        You are allowed to perform right shift operation any number of times.
    
    One right shift operation means shifting all elements of the array by one
    position in the right direction. The last element of the array will be moved to
    the starting position in the array i.e. 0th index. 

    If it is possible to obtain the sorted array by performing the above operation
    then return True else return False.
    If the given array is empty then return True.

    Note: The given list is guaranteed to have unique elements.

    For Example:
    
    move_one_ball([3, 4, 5, 1, 2])==>True
    Explanation: By performin 2 right shift operations, non-decreasing order can
                 be achieved for the given array.
    move_one_ball([3, 5, 4, 1, 2])==>False
    Explanation:It is not possible to get non-decreasing order for the given
                array by performing any number of right shift operations.
                
    """
    if not arr:
        return True
    for i in range(len(arr) - 1):
        if arr[i] > arr[i + 1]:
            return False
    return True

def exchange(lst1, lst2):
    """In this problem, you will implement a function that takes two lists of numbers,
    and determines whether it is possible to perform an exchange of elements
    between them to make lst1 a list of only even numbers.
    There is no limit on the number of exchanged elements between lst1 and lst2.
    If it is possible to exchange elements between the lst1 and lst2 to make
    all the elements of lst1 to be even, return "YES".
    Otherwise, return "NO".
    For example:
    exchange([1, 2, 3, 4], [1, 2, 3, 4]) => "YES"
    exchange([1, 2, 3, 4], [1, 5, 3, 4]) => "NO"
    It is assumed that the input lists will be non-empty.
    """
    # Check if all elements in lst1 are even
    if all(num % 2 == 0 for num in lst1):
        return "YES"
    # Check if all elements in lst2 are even
    elif all(num % 2 == 0 for num in lst2):
        return "YES"
    # If neither lst1 nor lst2 contains all even numbers, return "NO"
    else:
        return "NO"

def exchange(lst1, lst2):
    """In this problem, you will implement a function that takes two lists of numbers,
    and determines whether it is possible to perform an exchange of elements
    between them to make lst1 a list of only even numbers.
    There is no limit on the number of exchanged elements between lst1 and lst2.
    If it is possible to exchange elements between the lst1 and lst2 to make
    all the elements of lst1 to be even, return "YES".
    Otherwise, return "NO".
    For example:
    exchange([1, 2, 3, 4], [1, 2, 3, 4]) => "YES"
    exchange([1, 2, 3, 4], [1, 5, 3, 4]) => "NO"
    It is assumed that the input lists will be non-empty.
    """
    even_count_lst1 = sum(1 for num in lst1 if num % 2 == 0)
    even_count_lst2 = sum(1 for num in lst2 if num % 2 == 0)
    
    if even_count_lst1 + even_count_lst2 >= len(lst1):
        return "YES"
    else:
        return "NO"

入口函数：histogram

人工静态审阅：DPO 处理空文本并给 max 设置默认值；SFT 对空计数直接求 max。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
def histogram(test):
    """Given a string representing a space separated lowercase letters, return a dictionary
    of the letter with the most repetition and containing the corresponding count.
    If several letters have the same occurrence, return all of them.
    
    Example:
    histogram('a b c') == {'a': 1, 'b': 1, 'c': 1}
    histogram('a b b a') == {'a': 2, 'b': 2}
    histogram('a b c a b') == {'a': 2, 'b': 2}
    histogram('b b b b a') == {'b': 4}
    histogram('') == {}

    """

LoRA SFT · base=fail / plus=fail

原始 completion · 906 字符（不是 token 数）

    # Split the string into a list of letters
    letters = test.split()
    
    # Create a dictionary to store the count of each letter
    letter_count = {}
    
    # Iterate over each letter in the list
    for letter in letters:
        # If the letter is already in the dictionary, increment its count
        if letter in letter_count:
            letter_count[letter] += 1
        # Otherwise, add the letter to the dictionary with a count of 1
        else:
            letter_count[letter] = 1
    
    # Find the letter with the maximum count
    max_count = max(letter_count.values())
    
    # Create a list of letters with the maximum count
    max_letters = [letter for letter, count in letter_count.items() if count == max_count]
    
    # Return the dictionary of letters with the maximum count
    return {letter: count for letter, count in letter_count.items() if count == max_count}
完整被评分代码 solution
def histogram(test):
    """Given a string representing a space separated lowercase letters, return a dictionary
    of the letter with the most repetition and containing the corresponding count.
    If several letters have the same occurrence, return all of them.
    
    Example:
    histogram('a b c') == {'a': 1, 'b': 1, 'c': 1}
    histogram('a b b a') == {'a': 2, 'b': 2}
    histogram('a b c a b') == {'a': 2, 'b': 2}
    histogram('b b b b a') == {'b': 4}
    histogram('') == {}

    """
    # Split the string into a list of letters
    letters = test.split()
    
    # Create a dictionary to store the count of each letter
    letter_count = {}
    
    # Iterate over each letter in the list
    for letter in letters:
        # If the letter is already in the dictionary, increment its count
        if letter in letter_count:
            letter_count[letter] += 1
        # Otherwise, add the letter to the dictionary with a count of 1
        else:
            letter_count[letter] = 1
    
    # Find the letter with the maximum count
    max_count = max(letter_count.values())
    
    # Create a list of letters with the maximum count
    max_letters = [letter for letter, count in letter_count.items() if count == max_count]
    
    # Return the dictionary of letters with the maximum count
    return {letter: count for letter, count in letter_count.items() if count == max_count}

def histogram(test):
    """Given a string representing a space separated lowercase letters, return a dictionary
    of the letter with the most repetition and containing the corresponding count.
    If several letters have the same occurrence, return all of them.
    
    Example:
    histogram('a b c') == {'a': 1, 'b': 1, 'c': 1}
    histogram('a b b a') == {'a': 2, 'b': 2}
    histogram('a b c a b') == {'a': 2, 'b': 2}
    histogram('b b b b a') == {'b': 4}

            letter_count[letter] = 1
    
    max_count = max(letter_count.values(), default=0)
    result = {letter: count for letter, count in letter_count.items() if count == max_count}
    
    return result

def match_parens(lst):
    '''
    You are given a list of two strings, both strings consist of open
    parentheses '(' or close parentheses ')' only.
    Your job is to check if it is possible to concatenate the two strings in
    some order, that the resulting string will be good.
    A string S is considered to be good if and only if all parentheses in S
    are balanced. For example: the string '(())()' is good, while the string
    '())' is not.
    Return 'Yes' if there's a way to make a good string, and return 'No' otherwise.

    Examples:
    match_parens(['()(', ')']) == 'Yes'
    match_parens([')', ')']) == 'No'
    '''

    def is_good(s):
        stack = []
        for char in s:
            if char == '(':
                stack.append(char)
            else:
                if not stack:
                    return False
                stack.pop()
        return not stack

    for i in range(2):
        if is_good(lst[i]):
            return 'Yes'
    return 'No'
完整被评分代码 solution
def match_parens(lst):
    '''
    You are given a list of two strings, both strings consist of open
    parentheses '(' or close parentheses ')' only.
    Your job is to check if it is possible to concatenate the two strings in
    some order, that the resulting string will be good.
    A string S is considered to be good if and only if all parentheses in S
    are balanced. For example: the string '(())()' is good, while the string
    '())' is not.
    Return 'Yes' if there's a way to make a good string, and return 'No' otherwise.

    Examples:
    match_parens(['()(', ')']) == 'Yes'
    match_parens([')', ')']) == 'No'
    '''
    def is_good(s):
        stack = []
        for char in s:
            if char == '(':
                stack.append(char)
            else:
                if not stack:
                    return False
                stack.pop()
        return not stack

    for i in range(2):
        if is_good(lst[i]):
            return 'Yes'
    return 'No'

完整被评分代码 solution
def match_parens(lst):
    '''
    You are given a list of two strings, both strings consist of open
    parentheses '(' or close parentheses ')' only.
    Your job is to check if it is possible to concatenate the two strings in
    some order, that the resulting string will be good.
    A string S is considered to be good if and only if all parentheses in S
    are balanced. For example: the string '(())()' is good, while the string
    '())' is not.
    Return 'Yes' if there's a way to make a good string, and return 'No' otherwise.

    Examples:
    match_parens(['()(', ')']) == 'Yes'
    match_parens([')', ')']) == 'No'
    '''
    def is_balanced(s):
        stack = []
        for char in s:
            if char == '(':
                stack.append(char)
            elif char == ')':
                if not stack:
                    return False
                stack.pop()
        return len(stack) == 0

    for order in [(lst[0], lst[1]), (lst[1], lst[0])]:
        if is_balanced(order[0] + order[1]):
            return 'Yes'
    return 'No'

def split_words(txt):
    '''
    Given a string of words, return a list of words split on whitespace, if no whitespaces exists in the text you
    should split on commas ',' if no commas exists you should return the number of lower-case letters with odd order in the
    alphabet, ord('a') = 0, ord('b') = 1, ... ord('z') = 25
    Examples
    split_words("Hello world!") ➞ ["Hello", "world!"]
    split_words("Hello,world!") ➞ ["Hello", "world!"]
    split_words("abcdef") == 3 
    '''

原始 completion · 186 字符（不是 token 数）

    if ' ' in txt:
        return txt.split()
    elif ',' in txt:
        return txt.split(',')
    else:
        return sum(1 for i, c in enumerate(txt) if i % 2 == 0 and c.islower())
完整被评分代码 solution
def split_words(txt):
    '''
    Given a string of words, return a list of words split on whitespace, if no whitespaces exists in the text you
    should split on commas ',' if no commas exists you should return the number of lower-case letters with odd order in the
    alphabet, ord('a') = 0, ord('b') = 1, ... ord('z') = 25
    Examples
    split_words("Hello world!") ➞ ["Hello", "world!"]
    split_words("Hello,world!") ➞ ["Hello", "world!"]
    split_words("abcdef") == 3 
    '''
    if ' ' in txt:
        return txt.split()
    elif ',' in txt:
        return txt.split(',')
    else:
        return sum(1 for i, c in enumerate(txt) if i % 2 == 0 and c.islower())

完整被评分代码 solution
def split_words(txt):
    '''
    Given a string of words, return a list of words split on whitespace, if no whitespaces exists in the text you
    should split on commas ',' if no commas exists you should return the number of lower-case letters with odd order in the
    alphabet, ord('a') = 0, ord('b') = 1, ... ord('z') = 25
    Examples
    split_words("Hello world!") ➞ ["Hello", "world!"]
    split_words("Hello,world!") ➞ ["Hello", "world!"]
    split_words("abcdef") == 3 
    '''
    if ' ' in txt:
        return txt.split()
    elif ',' in txt:
        return txt.split(',')
    else:
        count = 0
        for char in txt:
            if 97 <= ord(char) <= 122:
                if (ord(char) - 97) % 2 == 1:
                    count += 1
        return count

完整被评分代码 solution
def simplify(x, n):
    """Your task is to implement a function that will simplify the expression
    x * n. The function returns True if x * n evaluates to a whole number and False
    otherwise. Both x and n, are string representation of a fraction, and have the following format,
    <numerator>/<denominator> where both numerator and denominator are positive whole numbers.

    You can assume that x, and n are valid fractions, and do not have zero as denominator.

    simplify("1/5", "5/1") = True
    simplify("1/6", "2/1") = False
    simplify("7/10", "10/2") = False
    """
    x = x.split('/')
    n = n.split('/')
    x = int(x[0]) / int(x[1])
    n = int(n[0]) / int(n[1])
    return x * n == int(x * n)

完整被评分代码 solution
def simplify(x, n):
    """Your task is to implement a function that will simplify the expression
    x * n. The function returns True if x * n evaluates to a whole number and False
    otherwise. Both x and n, are string representation of a fraction, and have the following format,
    <numerator>/<denominator> where both numerator and denominator are positive whole numbers.

    You can assume that x, and n are valid fractions, and do not have zero as denominator.

    simplify("1/5", "5/1") = True
    simplify("1/6", "2/1") = False
    simplify("7/10", "10/2") = False
    """
    numerator_x, denominator_x = map(int, x.split('/'))
    numerator_n, denominator_n = map(int, n.split('/'))
    
    product_numerator = numerator_x * numerator_n
    product_denominator = denominator_x * denominator_n
    
    return product_numerator % product_denominator == 0

def double_the_difference(lst):
    '''
    Given a list of numbers, return the sum of squares of the numbers
    in the list that are odd. Ignore numbers that are negative or not integers.
    
    double_the_difference([1, 3, 2, 0]) == 1 + 9 + 0 + 0 = 10
    double_the_difference([-1, -2, 0]) == 0
    double_the_difference([9, -2]) == 81
    double_the_difference([0]) == 0  
   
    If the input list is empty, return 0.
    '''
    return sum(x**2 for x in lst if x > 0 and x % 2 != 0)

def double_the_difference(lst):
    '''
    Given a list of numbers, return the sum of squares of the numbers
    in the list that are odd. Ignore numbers that are negative or not integers.
    
    double_the_difference([1, 3, 2, 0]) == 1 + 9 + 0 + 0 = 10
    double_the_difference([-1, -2, 0]) == 0
    double_the_difference([9, -2]) == 81
    double_the_difference([0]) == 0  
   
    If the input list is empty, return 0.
    '''
    if not lst:
        return 0
    
    return sum(x**2 for x in lst if isinstance(x, int) and x > 0 and x % 2 != 0)

def find_Rotations(s):
    """
    Finds the minimum number of rotations greater than 0 required to get the same string.
    
    Parameters:
    s (str): The input string.
    
    Returns:
    int: The minimum number of rotations.
    """
    n = len(s)
    for i in range(1, n):
        if s[i:] + s[:i] == s:
            return i
    return 0

Mbpp/12 · 改善 题意与规则

入口函数：sort_matrix

人工静态审阅：DPO 按行和排序行，SFT 排序每行内部元素。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a function to sort a given matrix in ascending order according to the sum of its rows.
assert sort_matrix([[1, 2, 3], [2, 4, 5], [1, 1, 1]])==[[1, 1, 1], [1, 2, 3], [2, 4, 5]]
"""

LoRA SFT · base=fail / plus=fail

原始 completion · 296 字符（不是 token 数）

def sort_matrix(matrix):
    """
    Sorts a given matrix in ascending order according to the sum of its rows.

    Parameters:
    matrix (list of list of int): The matrix to be sorted.

    Returns:
    list of list of int: The sorted matrix.
    """
    return [sorted(row) for row in matrix]
完整被评分代码 solution
"""
Write a function to sort a given matrix in ascending order according to the sum of its rows.
assert sort_matrix([[1, 2, 3], [2, 4, 5], [1, 1, 1]])==[[1, 1, 1], [1, 2, 3], [2, 4, 5]]
"""
def sort_matrix(matrix):

    Sorts a given matrix in ascending order according to the sum of its rows.

    Parameters:
    matrix (list of list of int): The matrix to be sorted.

    Returns:
    list of list of int: The sorted matrix.
    """
    return [sorted(row) for row in matrix]

def sort_matrix(matrix):
    """
    Sorts a given matrix in ascending order based on the sum of its rows.

    Parameters:
    matrix (list of list of int): The matrix to be sorted.

    Returns:
    list of list of int: The sorted matrix.
    """
    # Calculate the sum of each row
    row_sums = [sum(row) for row in matrix]
    
    # Sort the matrix based on the row sums
    sorted_matrix = [row for _, row in sorted(zip(row_sums, matrix))]
    
    return sorted_matrix

Mbpp/67 · 改善 算法逻辑

入口函数：bell_number

人工静态审阅：DPO 的 Bell 数递推加入二项式系数；SFT 递推求的是不同的序列。与 Mbpp/608 的退化并存，不能断言 DPO 一概改善或损伤 Bell 数题。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a function to find the number of ways to partition a set of Bell numbers.
assert bell_number(2)==2
"""

LoRA SFT · base=fail / plus=fail

原始 completion · 413 字符（不是 token 数）

def bell_number(n):
    """
    Calculate the number of ways to partition a set of n elements using Bell numbers.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): The number of elements in the set.
    
    Returns:
    int: The number of ways to partition the set.
    """
    if n == 0:
        return 1
    elif n == 1:
        return 1
    else:
        return sum(bell_number(i) * bell_number(n - i - 1) for i in range(n))

def bell_number(n):
    """
    Calculate the nth Bell number using dynamic programming.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): The number of elements in the set.
    
    Returns:
    int: The nth Bell number.
    """
    if n == 0:
        return 1
    bell = [0] * (n + 1)
    bell[0] = 1
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        bell[i] = sum(bell[j] * binomial_coefficient(i - 1, j) for j in range(i))
    return bell[n]

def binomial_coefficient(n, k):
    """
    Calculate the binomial coefficient C(n, k) using dynamic programming.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): The total number of items.
    k (int): The number of items to choose.
    
    Returns:
    int: The binomial coefficient C(n, k).
    """
    if k > n:
        return 0
    if k == 0 or k == n:
        return 1
    C = [0] * (k + 1)
    C[0] = 1
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        for j in range(min(i, k), 0, -1):
            C[j] += C[j - 1]
    return C[k]

def is_sublist(lst, sublst):
    """
    Check if the given list contains the given sublist.

    Parameters:
    lst (list): The list to be checked.
    sublst (list): The sublist to be found in the list.

    Returns:
    bool: True if the sublist is found in the list, False otherwise.
    """
    return all(item in lst for item in sublst)

原始 completion · 481 字符（不是 token 数）

def is_sublist(main_list, sub_list):
    """
    Check if the sub_list is a sublist of the main_list.

    :param main_list: List to search within.
    :param sub_list: List to search for as a sublist.
    :return: True if sub_list is a sublist of main_list, False otherwise.
    """
    if not sub_list:
        return True
    if not main_list:
        return False
    if main_list[:len(sub_list)] == sub_list:
        return True
    return is_sublist(main_list[1:], sub_list)

入口函数：maximize_elements

人工静态审阅：DPO 对内部 tuple 的对应元素取最大值；SFT 对整个 tuple 做字典序 max。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a function to maximize the given two tuples.
assert maximize_elements(((1, 3), (4, 5), (2, 9), (1, 10)), ((6, 7), (3, 9), (1, 1), (7, 3))) == ((6, 7), (4, 9), (2, 9), (7, 10))
"""

LoRA SFT · base=fail / plus=fail

原始 completion · 487 字符（不是 token 数）

def maximize_elements(tuples1, tuples2):
    """
    Maximizes the elements of two tuples of tuples.
    
    Parameters:
    tuples1 (tuple of tuples): The first tuple of tuples.
    tuples2 (tuple of tuples): The second tuple of tuples.
    
    Returns:
    tuple of tuples: A tuple containing the maximized elements from both input tuples.
    """
    max_elements = []
    for t1, t2 in zip(tuples1, tuples2):
        max_elements.append(max(t1, t2))
    return tuple(max_elements)

原始 completion · 557 字符（不是 token 数）

def maximize_elements(tuples1, tuples2):
    """
    Maximizes the elements of two tuples element-wise.
    
    Parameters:
    tuples1 (tuple of tuples): The first tuple of tuples.
    tuples2 (tuple of tuples): The second tuple of tuples.
    
    Returns:
    tuple of tuples: A new tuple of tuples with the maximum elements from each pair.
    """
    max_tuples = []
    for t1, t2 in zip(tuples1, tuples2):
        max_tuple = tuple(max(pair1, pair2) for pair1, pair2 in zip(t1, t2))
        max_tuples.append(max_tuple)
    return tuple(max_tuples)

Mbpp/274 · 改善 性能

入口函数：even_binomial_Coeff_Sum

人工静态审阅：DPO 用 math.comb 替代无缓存的二项式递归，避免大量重复计算；具体测试失败是否由超时造成未重新核实。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a python function that takes in a positive integer n and finds the sum of even index binomial coefficients.
assert even_binomial_Coeff_Sum(4) == 8
"""

LoRA SFT · base=pass / plus=fail

原始 completion · 849 字符（不是 token 数）

def even_binomial_Coeff_Sum(n):
    """
    Calculate the sum of even index binomial coefficients for a given positive integer n.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): A positive integer representing the number of terms in the binomial expansion.
    
    Returns:
    int: The sum of even index binomial coefficients.
    """
    sum_even = 0
    for i in range(0, n+1, 2):
        sum_even += binomial_coefficient(n, i)
    return sum_even

def binomial_coefficient(n, k):
    """
    Calculate the binomial coefficient for given n and k.
    
    Parameters:
    n (int): The total number of items.
    k (int): The number of items to choose.
    
    Returns:
    int: The binomial coefficient.
    """
    if k > n:
        return 0
    if k == 0 or k == n:
        return 1
    return binomial_coefficient(n-1, k-1) + binomial_coefficient(n-1, k)
完整被评分代码 solution

    Returns:
    int: The sum of even index binomial coefficients.
    """
    sum_even = 0

        sum_even += binomial_coefficient(n, i)
    return sum_even

Mbpp/409 · 改善 顶层断言

入口函数：min_product_tuple

人工静态审阅：两个目标函数主体相同。SFT 顶层自写断言把 [(10,20),(15,2),(5,10)] 的最小乘积写成 10，实际为 30；DPO 没有追加这些断言。静态算术支持改善来自删除干扰，而非函数算法提升。

题目 prompt（点击展开）
"""
Write a function to find the minimum product from the pairs of tuples within a given list.
assert min_product_tuple([(2, 7), (2, 6), (1, 8), (4, 9)] )==8
"""

LoRA SFT · base=fail / plus=fail

原始 completion · 656 字符（不是 token 数）

def min_product_tuple(tuples_list):
    """
    Finds the minimum product from the pairs of tuples within a given list.

    Parameters:
    tuples_list (list of tuples): A list containing tuples of integers.

    Returns:
    int: The minimum product from the pairs of tuples.
    """
    min_product = float('inf')
    for a, b in tuples_list:
        product = a * b
        if product < min_product:
            min_product = product
    return min_product

# Test cases
assert min_product_tuple([(2, 7), (2, 6), (1, 8), (4, 9)]) == 8
assert min_product_tuple([(10, 20), (15, 2), (5, 10)]) == 10
assert min_product_tuple([(1, 1), (2, 2), (3, 3)]) == 1

Mbpp/579 · 改善 题意与规则

入口函数：find_dissimilar

人工静态审阅：DPO 求两个集合的对称差；SFT 只求第一个 tuple 中不属于第二个的元素。

Write a function to find the dissimilar elements in the given two tuples.

"""

def find_dissimilar(tup1, tup2):
    """
    Finds and returns a tuple containing the elements that are present in the first tuple but not in the second tuple.
    
    :param tup1: First input tuple
    :param tup2: Second input tuple
    :return: Tuple of dissimilar elements
    """
    return tuple(element for element in tup1 if element not in tup2)
完整被评分代码 solution
"""
Write a function to find the dissimilar elements in the given two tuples.

"""
def find_dissimilar(tup1, tup2):
    """
    Finds and returns a tuple containing the elements that are present in the first tuple but not in the second tuple.
    
    :param tup1: First input tuple
    :param tup2: Second input tuple
    :return: Tuple of dissimilar elements
    """
    return tuple(element for element in tup1 if element not in tup2)

原始 completion · 465 字符（不是 token 数）

def find_dissimilar(tup1, tup2):
    """
    Finds and returns the dissimilar elements between two tuples.
    
    Parameters:
    tup1 (tuple): The first tuple of elements.
    tup2 (tuple): The second tuple of elements.
    
    Returns:
    tuple: A tuple containing the dissimilar elements from both input tuples.
    """
    set1 = set(tup1)
    set2 = set(tup2)
    dissimilar_elements = set1.symmetric_difference(set2)
    return tuple(dissimilar_elements)

DPO · base=pass / plus=pass

原始 completion · 485 字符（不是 token 数）

def reverse_Array_Upto_K(arr, k):
    """
    Reverses the elements of the array up to the given position k.
    
    Parameters:
    arr (list): The list of integers to be reversed.
    k (int): The position up to which the array should be reversed.
    
    Returns:
    list: The reversed array up to the position k.
    """
    left, right = 0, k - 1
    while left < right:
        arr[left], arr[right] = arr[right], arr[left]
        left += 1
        right -= 1
    return arr

def rotate_right(lst, n):
    """
    Rotates the list to the right by n items.
    
    Parameters:
    lst (list): The list to be rotated.
    n (int): The number of items to rotate the list by.

    list: The rotated list.
    """

    Rotates the list 'lst' to the right by 'n' positions.
    
    Parameters:
    lst (list): The list to be rotated.
    n (int): The number of positions to rotate the list to the right.
    
    Returns:
    list: The rotated list.
    """

    n = n % len(lst)
    return lst[-n:] + lst[:-n]

Mbpp/784 · 改善 题意与规则

def mul_even_odd(lst):
    """
    Calculate the product of the first even and odd numbers in a list.

    Parameters:
    lst (list): A list of integers.

    Returns:
    int: The product of the first even and odd numbers in the list.
    """
    even = 1
    odd = 1
    for num in lst:
        if num % 2 == 0:
            even *= num
        else:
            odd *= num
    return even * odd

def mul_even_odd(lst):
    """
    Calculate the product of the first even and odd numbers in a list.
    
    Parameters:
    lst (list): A list of integers.
    
    Returns:
    int: The product of the first even and odd numbers, or 1 if there are no such numbers.
    """
    even = [x for x in lst if x % 2 == 0]
    odd = [x for x in lst if x % 2 != 0]
    
    if even and odd:
        return even[0] * odd[0]
    else:
        return 1
